| `jig:stop-gate` | <a id="r2-part-jig-stop-gate"></a>Stop gate | Holds the agent's turn open until the deterministic suite passes on its diff: tests, diff-scoped analysis, secret scanning, the dependency policy against the mirror, authorization-pattern rules and derived checks. | Harness stop hook that runs the deterministic suite on the turn's diff |  | shared:existing |  |  | existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:quarantined-reader` | <a id="r2-part-jig-quarantined-reader"></a>Quarantined reader for outside text | The only place in the loop where outside text is read by a model: issue and review text, web pages, public issue threads and dependency documentation. A tool-less model session with no repository access, no write path and no shared context with the coding agent. It answers the agent's typed question in the question's schema, and its answer reaches the agent only through the reader's schema gate. | Tool-less model session with a fixed output schema per question |  | shared:existing |  |  | swap point | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:reader-schema-gate` | <a id="r2-part-jig-reader-schema-gate"></a>Reader schema gate | Validates each reader answer against its question's schema with no model call, and passes only typed fields: package and version identifiers, API names and signatures, error codes and links. Prose does not pass; an agent that needs prose from outside asks the developer, who reads it. | Schema validator with no model call | JSON Schema validator, Ajv | shared:existing |  |  | swap point | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:model-routes` | <a id="r2-part-jig-model-routes"></a>Model routes for coding agents and reviewers | The approved model endpoints the harnesses, the AI reviewer, triage, the hunk-risk advisor and autofix call, each call in its own session. No outside text reaches these sessions, so a response carries no outsider label. The quarantined reader calls a route of its own. | Approved hosted or local model endpoints for coding tools |  | shared:third-party |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:reader-model-route` | <a id="r2-part-jig-reader-model-route"></a>Model route for the quarantined reader | The model route the quarantined reader calls, kept apart from the routes the coding agent and the reviewers call: its own credentials and sessions, no tools, no knowledge base and no context shared with any other session. Its requests carry outside text, so every response it returns keeps the outsider label of the request it answers. | A separate model endpoint, credentials and sessions for reading outside text |  | shared:third-party |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:repository` | <a id="r2-part-jig-repository"></a>Repositories and mirrored dependencies | The organization's repositories as merged through the protected branch, and the dependencies admitted to its mirrors, as the session's checkout sees them. | Source control with protected branches, and a dependency mirror | Gitea, Forgejo, Sonatype Nexus Repository | shared:existing |  |  | existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:change-boundary` | <a id="r2-part-jig-change-boundary"></a>Commit and push boundary | Where each change is checked again with the same suite, stamped with its provenance and proposed for merge. | Commit, push and merge controls in source control |  | shared:existing |  |  | existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:precommit-gate` | <a id="r2-part-jig-precommit-gate"></a>Locked pre-commit gate | Runs the same suite at the same versions on each commit, from a hook path set and locked by endpoint management. For a harness without a stop gate, it is where the suite runs in the stop gate's place. | Pre-commit hook runner on a hook path locked by endpoint management | pre-commit, Lefthook | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:provenance-stamper` | <a id="r2-part-jig-provenance-stamper"></a>Provenance stamper | Attaches a signed provenance stamp to each change from the harness's session telemetry: tool and version, model and version, session and skill-bundle hash. A change from a harness that exports no telemetry carries no stamp and counts as unlabeled. The stamp feeds measurement and routing and grants no trust. | Signed commit trailer or attestation from session telemetry | in-toto attestations, Sigstore gitsign | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:protected-branch` | <a id="r2-part-jig-protected-branch"></a>Protected branch and server-side required checks | The only way a change merges: the same suite runs server-side as required checks that the agent cannot skip or reconfigure, and a person who is neither the agent nor its session's developer approves and merges. High-risk hunks also need the sign-off of a qualified person outside the authoring team. Its settings are a requirement, and a declared premise with a test and a fallback: each required check is tied to the app that reports it, the rule is enforced for repository administrators, and no agent identity holds administrator rights or any way to set a check's status. The backstop for a skipped local check and the fallback for every endpoint assumption. | Branch protection with server-side required checks and required independent review | Gitea branch protection, Forgejo Actions | shared:existing |  |  | existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |

| `jig:pr-analysis` | <a id="r2-part-jig-pr-analysis"></a>Diff-scoped deep analysis | Runs deep data-flow analysis scoped to the diff of each proposed change, with no model call. Its findings are leads, held to an effective false-positive rate under the bar the organization sets. | Diff-scoped data-flow static analysis | CodeQL, Semgrep | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:pr-triage` | <a id="r2-part-jig-pr-triage"></a>Model triage of pull-request findings | Labels each analysis finding for the reviewer's view. A label orders what the reviewer reads first; it never removes a finding from a hunk routed to a person, and it travels to Ratchet as a label on the lead. | Model triage of static-analysis findings, as labels |  | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:ai-reviewer` | <a id="r2-part-jig-ai-reviewer"></a>AI reviewer from a second model family | Reviews each diff with a model family other than the author's. Its comments are leads and never the independent review. | Model code review from a second model family |  | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:hunk-router` | <a id="r2-part-jig-hunk-router"></a>Hunk router | Routes each hunk by rule, with no model call: a hunk whose paths, owners or rule matches touch authorization, cryptography, data access or money goes to a qualified person with the spec and findings attached, and every other hunk goes to the lighter pass. The hunk-risk advisor may add hunks to the human route; nothing it sends can move a hunk off it. | Rule-based review routing by path, owner and rule match | CODEOWNERS, Danger JS | shared:existing |  |  | swap point, existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:hunk-risk-advisor` | <a id="r2-part-jig-hunk-risk-advisor"></a>Hunk-risk advisor | A model that reads the hunks the rules left on the lighter pass and names any it judges high-risk, with its reason. Its output can only add a hunk to the human route. | Model that flags hunks for human review, add-only |  | shared:existing |  |  | swap point | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |
| `jig:autofix` | <a id="r2-part-jig-autofix"></a>Autofix | Proposes patches for analysis findings and abstains when unsure. | `jig:measurement-plane` | <a id="r2-part-jig-measurement-plane"></a>Measurement plane and layer log | Logs where each layer caught what, sends findings to Ratchet as leads before triage with the dropped-check log, and reports outcomes to the people who decide: escaped defects per change by origin on the reproduced tier, reviewer approval drift, catch rate per layer and per rule, each on cohorts whose reproduction window has closed. The layer that should have caught each defect comes from Ratchet's evaluation group, never from this plane. It shows the policy-owning team each derived check's misses against its floor as Ratchet counted them, beside its own log of checks dropped or disabled; it never receives which changes missed or what a replay found. For class tests it receives and reports only rule id, rule version and pass or fail, never per-class counts; those go from the regression catalog directly to the policy-owning team: people, never an agent. | Layer log and outcome reporting over the shared record | Apache Superset, Metabase | shared:existing |  |  | swap point | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |

Anyone may have written it. | Public web content, issue threads and dependency documentation |  | shared:third-party |  |  | existing tool first | [module: Secure creation (Jig)](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-secure-creation) |

These remain the change and deployment path. | The organization's tracker, SCM, CI/CD and change management |  | shared:existing |  |  | existing tool first | [core: integration plane](R0-building-blocks.md#r0-block-core-shared:integration) |

<a id="r2-roles-people-hold"></a>
## Roles people hold

| Code | Role | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| `people.accountable-authorities` | <a id="r2-role-people-accountable-authorities"></a>Accountable people and policy | Engineering, security, IAM, SOC and risk: define scope, approve effects, accept evidence. |
| `people.engineering-owners` | <a id="r2-role-people-engineering-owners"></a>Engineering owners | Review / CI / deploy correction; valid deployed-state retest. |
| `people.independent-security-staff` | <a id="r2-role-people-independent-security-staff"></a>Independent security staff | Challenge proof and scope; accept closure / retained unknowns. |
| `people.risk-board-reporting` | <a id="r2-role-people-risk-board-reporting"></a>Risk reporting to the board | Assurance, exceptions and limits; formal testing remains separate. |
| `people.risk-acceptance-owner` | <a id="r2-role-people-risk-acceptance-owner"></a>Business risk owner (risk acceptance owner) | The business owner accountable for the unit's financial results, named and accountable. Accepts residual business risk, including attributed, expiring ship-through decisions on critical and crown-jewel paths (with security risk owner concurrence by default; a profile may change who signs). Not a verified technical path break. Must not be the discoverer, proposer or verifier of the finding it accepts. |
| `people.line-of-business-owner` | <a id="r2-role-people-line-of-business-owner"></a>Business owner (asset owner) | Accountable leader of a business unit or other organizational unit, accountable for the unit's financial results, with the engineering chain that supports it reporting beneath. Confirms or corrects the consequence tier the testing team drafts for each asset and service in that business unit; never drafts from scratch. Usually also holds the business risk owner role, which a profile may assign elsewhere. |
| `people.security-risk-owner` | <a id="r2-role-people-security-risk-owner"></a>Security risk owner | The security function or a risk function independent of the teams that build and run the system (the second line in the three-lines model). Sets the gate failure posture thresholds in the organization profile and concurs on every ship-through acceptance for critical and crown-jewel paths. Never the proposer of the test it governs. |
| `people.control-plane-operator` | <a id="r2-role-people-control-plane-operator"></a>Control-plane operator | Role in the assurance unit that operates the control plane: authorization service, policy engine, grant store, credential leases, model-boundary gates, protected audit, evidence stores and signing keys. Administrative access only through the privileged access broker under dual control; never holds the producer, platform-operator or verifier role. Usually part of an existing platform security or SRE job; dedicating people is the organization's choice. |
| `people.scenario-owner` | <a id="r2-role-people-scenario-owner"></a>Scenario owner | Owns one scenario: chooses it (by hand, from a lead, or from the existing discovery practice), names who owns its scope, fix and acceptance, and writes and signs its proof condition, often correcting a draft from the proof-condition drafter. Sometimes in security, sometimes the service or business owner. Never the team that drafts the scenario's tests. |
| `people.purple-team` | <a id="r2-role-people-purple-team"></a>Purple team | Joins attack and defense around validated findings. Its AI turns each validated finding into a proposed detection for the defensive side and a proposed kept regression test, runs the scheduled re-proof of kept tests against their fixes reverted, and supplies known-bad proposals and changes for the seed sets at the approval and review gates. Everything it produces is a proposal: a kept test enters the catalog only after independent retest and verifier acceptance, a re-proof result of "no longer fails" moves a test only after the verifier confirms it, a detection only after the defensive side's own review, and a seed only as the sampling service draws and places it. It may be the team that runs the agents. Never the verifier team, the team that owns proof conditions, the security risk owner or the defensive team that accepts detections, so it never accepts the kept tests, re-proof results, retirements or detections its own AI proposes. |
| `people.held-out-assessors` | <a id="r2-role-people-held-out-assessors"></a>Held-out assessors | A team outside Ratchet, internal or external, that reports to neither the assurance unit nor the team that runs the agents. Runs a recurring exercise against Ratchet's own control and evidence planes (the authorization service, grant store, credential leases, model-boundary gates, release pipeline, key management, evidence stores and the privileged access broker), under scope and rules the security risk owner approves, at least once a year and after any change that widens what the control plane allows. Its findings enter the record like any other finding: intake, independent reproduction and disposition, with the team that owns the part fixing it. Never a producer team, the verifier team or the control-plane operators. |
| `jig:people.creation-developer` | <a id="r2-role-jig-people-creation-developer"></a>Developer whose session runs the coding agent | Gives the coding agent its task and, for high-risk work, writes or signs the spec its acceptance tests derive from. Co-author of every change the session produces, so never the person who merges it. |
| `jig:people.change-reviewer` | <a id="r2-role-jig-people-change-reviewer"></a>Reviewer who merges agent-written changes | Reviews and merges a change written by an agent and a developer, being neither. Reads the triage-labeled findings, the AI reviewer's comments and the lighter-pass hunks. |
| `jig:people.high-risk-reviewer` | <a id="r2-role-jig-people-high-risk-reviewer"></a>Qualified reviewer of high-risk hunks | A person outside the authoring team, qualified for the area, who signs off each hunk routed to a person, with the spec and findings attached. |
| `jig:people.creation-policy-owner` | <a id="r2-role-jig-people-creation-policy-owner"></a>Policy-owning team | Owns the one policy source and the locked configuration it compiles into every harness. Turns each proven class into a rule, or records why it cannot yet, and has each rule tested against the class's reproduced members. Publishes the skills and instruction files for each paved road. Receives per-class test totals directly from the regression catalog for the policy-owning team: people, never an agent. Keeps those counts out of every agent-reachable part, published rule, guidance, report and model route. |

<a id="r2-separations-of-duties"></a>
## Separations of duties

| Rule it serves | Kept apart |
|---|---|
| A proposal is never permission | The team that drafts tests never approves their actions |
| A proposal is never permission | The tool that proposes is never the tool that authorizes |
| Proof conditions stay out of the testing agent's reach | The proof-condition owner is never the team that drafts tests |
| Acceptance is independent | The verifier is never the team that drafts tests |
| Acceptance is independent | The team that writes a fix never accepts its closure |
| Acceptance is independent | The business risk owner is neither the proposer nor the verifier |
| Acceptance is independent | The security risk owner is never the proposer |
| Consequence attestation and gate acceptance | The testing team drafts consequence tiers and the asset owner confirms them |
| Consequence attestation and gate acceptance | Ship-through acceptance and concurrence are different roles |
| Acceptance is independent | A tool's owner never computes that tool's yield |
| Acceptance is independent | The road owner never verifies its own road entries |
| Acceptance is independent | The road owner never computes road metrics |
| A proposal is never permission | The control-plane operator is never the platform operator |
| Acceptance is independent | The control-plane operator is neither the producer nor the verifier |

Lead refs: ids of tool leads that pointed at the hypothesis, provenance only. | ValidationArtifact | Integrity digest, target/fixture/data scope, source and disposition, original and patched test receipts; artifact type, origin, reproduced variants. Carries its outsider label set (OutsiderLabelSet), attached when it enters evidence from the request-data gate's records for the run that produced it: payloads, steps and seeds a producer wrote carry the run's labels. | at-least-once: content-addressed; duplicates collapse | s2.producer-adapters, s2.hybrid-source-producer, s2.runtime-assessment-producer, s2.workflow-worker-app | s2.artifact-exchange, s5.independent-verifier, s7.matched-evaluation-executor, s5.proof-evidence-service, s7.snapshot-cohort-manager |
| PatchCandidate | Proposed diff with target and fixture binding, original and patched outcomes, unresolved or failed tests; emitted as a reviewed proposal into existing engineering workflows, never self-merged. A seeded known-bad change for a reviewer-drift baseline does not travel as a PatchCandidate: its fixture binding and expected outcomes stay in the sampling service's frame, and only the change itself, as a SeededChange, goes to the engineering system and the reviewers. Carries its outsider label set (OutsiderLabelSet): a candidate a producer's model drafted carries its run's labels from the request-data gate's records, and a candidate the fix drafter writes from a fix brief carries the brief's labels, which a derived brief never has. For a co-created security correction, the one pull request includes the component-local instance regression and records security and owning-engineer participation. That visible test carries no proof condition, chained-path validation, protected fixture or aggregate expected outcome. A variant is a new finding through the normal loop and never silently expands this proposal. | at-least-once: content-addressed | s2.hybrid-source-producer, s4.response-proposal-gate, s6.owner-change-connector, s7.sampling-service, people.purple-team | s5.repair-checks, s6.enterprise-adapters, shared:ext.engineering-workflows, s7.sampling-service, s2.workflow-worker-app, s2.producer-adapters, s2.artifact-exchange, s6.paved-road-catalog |
| ActionSpec | Authenticated actor, reviewed primitive, bound target, normalized parameters with the version of the primitive's normalizer (the runner parses what it executes with the same version), the target's resolved address as checked at execution, prerequisite evidence, effect limits, approval binding, expiry, replay or idempotency control, policy version. A proposal confers no authority; the approval binding is set by the approval side, never by the proposer. | at-least-once: hash over normalized spec; duplicate submissions map to one approval | s2.producer-adapters, s2.workflow-worker-app, s4.response-proposal-gate, s6.retest-regression-planner, s7.sampling-service, s2.hybrid-source-producer, s2.runtime-assessment-producer | s3.action-broker, s3.action-control-service, s3.policy-decision-point, s3.approval-console, s2.workflow-worker-app, s2.producer-adapters |
| ExecutionGrant | Denial or short-lived exact grant bound to the ActionSpec fields; consumed atomically by the local enforcement point. | exactly-once consumption: atomic single-use consume in the grant store; replay denied; expiry against trusted time with skew margin | s3.policy-decision-point, s3.action-control-service | s3.local-enforcement-point, s3.grant-approval-store, s3.bounded-runner, s3.source-build-cell, s3.staging-cell, s3.production-cell |
| ExecutionReceipt | Effective target and identity, tested state, outcomes and telemetry, execution and policy versions, cleanup status, proof scope. Carries its outsider label set (OutsiderLabelSet) field by field: steps and payloads replayed from a producer's artifact keep that artifact's labels, and the target's responses are labeled as outsider text. On the way into evidence the independent verifier clears only the fields it constrains (the verdict, status classes, assertion results, references and hashes); every other field keeps its labels. | at-least-once: keyed by grant id; a grant has at most one terminal receipt; missing receipt means effect-uncertain | s3.bounded-runner, s3.observation-receipt, s3.staging-cell, s3.production-cell, s3.action-control-service | s5.evidence-intake-validator, s5.independent-verifier, s5.finding-ledger, s3.protected-audit, ext.soc-incident-response, s5.proof-evidence-service, s3.observation-receipt, s3.replay-verdict, s5.fix-brief-builder |
| FindingDisposition | Versioned disposition with proof mode, environment, assumptions, reviewer and risk acceptance if any. State: confirmed, rejected, unresolved or held. An unresolved disposition carries its reason. A reproduction that fails with no control observed stopping it is unresolved with reason reproduction failed, and is never rejected, because a reproduction can fail on a condition the report left out. Held: the independent reproduction ran in the deployed system and stopped short of the proof condition at a control its receipt observed refusing the step. The held record names the weakness, that control (the part, rule or setting and its version, as the receipt shows it) and the path up to it; the assets the proof condition names, with the consequence tier the business owner confirmed for them; and the id of its held-dependency test, the kept test that runs the path against that control and meets the proof condition only once the control stops holding. A held finding is neither exploitable nor closed: it is tracked as a resilience concern and never queued as exploitable, and the weakness still closes only through its kind's closure. Only a kind that declares held in the finding-kind registry admits a held disposition, and disposition review refuses one under another kind or with no held-dependency test. A held finding reads unresolved in every record and measure while its test is missing, has no owner or re-test interval, or has not run within that interval. When the test's run meets the proof condition, the finding becomes confirmed with the reach that run demonstrated, the tier recorded at hold and urgency read from the attacker-side signals, with no new triage. A control credited from a ticket, a diagram, a model's reasoning or a person's word, and never observed in a reproduction, holds nothing: the finding stays confirmed or unresolved as its reproduction showed. validation_basis: independent-reproduction, or self-validated with the AutonomyGrant id it ran under. A self-validated finding keeps that label in every record and measure, so each measure can be read with and without it. finding_kind, optional: the id of a kind in the model's finding-kind registry, absent meaning path. The shipped kinds are path (a weakness demonstrated through the running system), exposed-secret (a live credential found in code, history, logs, tickets or another store) and infra-misconfiguration (live infrastructure configuration that does not match its declared intent); an organization adds kinds by patch to the registry. A disposition naming an unregistered kind is refused at intake. The kind decides how the finding closes: its closure check, kept gate, recovery measure, and what never closes it. An exposed-secret finding is confirmed from the scan rule's hit and the credential's issuer and identifier, carries a fingerprint of the value and never the value, and is fixed by revoking and rotating the credential, which the owning team does through its fix path; its SecretRevocation record holds the fix and the check that closes it. Carries the outsider label set (OutsiderLabelSet) of its inputs field by field, less the fields the independent verifier or a person at the review console cleared, each with its clearing record. | at-least-once: keyed by finding id and version; later versions supersede, never overwrite | s5.disposition-review, s5.proof-evidence-service, s5.protected-evidence-store, s5.path-impact-graph | s5.finding-ledger, s6.owner-change-connector, s6.hardening-service, people.engineering-owners, people.independent-security-staff, people.risk-acceptance-owner, s1.context-compiler, s3.gate-failure-policy, s7.tool-yield-evaluator, s6.retest-regression-planner, s7.sampling-service, s5.path-impact-graph, s5.failure-class-registry, s3.approval-console, people.accountable-authorities, s1.tool-lead-store |
| RegressionSpec | Test id and version, invariant and context dependencies, declared dependencies at a fixed grain (the artifact digests it exercises, each library at its resolved version, each configuration key it reads, each policy id and identity), expected boundary behavior, observation projection (the fields of an observed outcome its verdict hashes, such as refused or allowed, status class and assertion results, and the fields it excludes, such as timestamps, session ids and ordering), authorized replay triggers, execution_class (static \| in-process \| runtime-replay) and expected_runtime. A kept regression with a static or in-process execution class may yield a DerivedCheck; the regression itself never leaves the loop. This protected record specifies security-owned aggregate validation, including proof conditions and chained paths. It is separate from the visible component-local InstanceRegression kept in the owning team's suite. Passing that local test cannot satisfy this record or replace independent verification. No separate variant probe set is maintained. | at-least-once: versioned by test id | s6.retest-regression-planner, s6.hardening-service | s6.regression-catalog |

A label set the producer supplies is ignored and logged. | at-most-once per attempt: retries create a new attempt with a new key; usage recorded per attempt | s2.producer-adapters, s2.workflow-worker-app, s4.request-data-gate, s2.quarantined-lead-reader, s2.typed-decision-worker, s2.proof-condition-drafter | s4.request-data-gate, s4.model-data-gateway, s4.route-budget-broker, s4.inference-endpoints, ext.hosted-frontier-endpoint |

The privileged access broker records each session to protected audit, with its requester, its second approver and any break-glass approval, before the session opens, and each command before it runs, under custody the operator cannot alter. | append-only record: session id; every command recorded before it runs; never edits evidence or grant content | s3.privileged-access-broker | s3.policy-decision-point, s3.grant-approval-store, s3.key-management, s5.protected-evidence-store, s4.request-data-gate, s4.response-proposal-gate, s3.protected-audit, s3.action-control-service, s5.proof-evidence-service, s1.live-precondition-resolver, s6.release-state-correlator, s6.retest-regression-planner, s6.road-conformance-check, s6.developer-queue-filter, s6.fix-cap-gate, s7.promotion-review, s7.assurance-incident-integration, s5.fixture-oracle-registry, s5.finding-ledger, s6.change-closure-records, s6.regression-catalog, s1.consequence-register, s5.path-impact-graph, s3.gate-failure-policy, s5.failure-class-registry, s5.road-assurance-record, s7.tool-portfolio-decisions, s7.dial-register, s6.fix-cap-refusal-log, s5.learning-store, s3.policy-release-pipeline, s3.privileged-access-broker, s6.kept-test-mirror |
| KeyReference | Key id, version, algorithm, public key or certificate chain, purpose (posture, bundle, lease, receipt, dial, limit, frame, snapshot, partition, restore, release, learned item, fix brief, revocation), partition when set, valid-from, signs-until, verifies-until (overlap window), revocation status. Private key material never leaves key management. | versioned artifact: key id plus version; overlapping validity windows; revocation propagates to every consumer within the posture expiry; a window bundle already minted sees a revocation only when it expires | s3.key-management | s3.gate-failure-policy, s3.credential-lease-broker, s3.observation-receipt, s7.dial-register, s1.context-compiler, s5.proof-evidence-service, s3.protected-audit, s3.local-enforcement-point, s6.enterprise-adapters, s3.policy-release-pipeline, s5.learning-store, s4.qualification-route-registry |
| ReplayRequest | Stage run id, service, deployed candidate digest, regression test ids, declared dependency references touched by the deploy, window bundle id used. Recorded for accounting and replay-owed tracking; the window bundle already authorizes the replay. | at-least-once: idempotency key = stage run id plus test id | s6.enterprise-adapters, s3.gate-failure-policy | s6.retest-regression-planner |
| ReplayWindowBundle | Bundle id, service, deploy window start and end, pre-authorized replay class and policy version, one single-use grant and scoped lease per declared regression test (ActionSpec hash bound), each test's registered expected-outcome hash, destination allowlist, signature and key reference. Verified offline by the local enforcement point; expires at window end. | signed artifact with expiry: one per service and deploy window; each grant inside is consumed once; verified offline; its revocation lag is its lifetime: a narrowed design limit or a revoked key reaches a bundle only when it expires, so the lifetime from minting to expiry is capped by window_bundle_max_hours, or by posture_expiry_hours while that dial is unset, and a longer bundle is never minted | s3.credential-lease-broker | s6.enterprise-adapters, s3.local-enforcement-point |
| ReplayVerdict | Stage run id, test id, grant id, verdict (pass \| fail \| unknown) with reason, observed-outcome hash over the test's observation projection, expected-outcome hash, receipt reference, cleanup status. Computed mechanically; never a model judgment. | at-least-once: keyed by stage run id, test id and grant id; one terminal verdict per grant | s3.replay-verdict | s6.enterprise-adapters, s5.evidence-intake-validator, s6.regression-catalog |
| AuditRecord | Record id, previous-record hash, grant or session id, action, target, time, outcome, cleanup status. Release admission and rollback records also carry cell_id, highest_verified_sequence, previous_running_sequence, admitted_sequence, release_digest and rollback_number, so each accepted older start can be matched to exactly one protected pipeline rollback event. Written before the effect; append-only. A weights release the route registry refuses is written as a record too, with the release as its target, the check that failed as its outcome and no grant. | append-only: hash-chained; written before the effect; drained exactly once by record id | s3.bounded-runner, s3.local-audit-buffer, s3.local-enforcement-point, s4.qualification-route-registry | s3.local-audit-buffer, s3.protected-audit |
| PolicyBundle | Bundle id and version, target (policy engine, model-boundary gate, enforcement point, route table, store schema), change as code, policy unit test results, deny-diff (what newly allows and what newly denies), widening flag and security risk owner approval when widening, rollout stage, rollback reference, signature and key reference. | versioned artifact: bundle id plus version; staged; rollback re-releases the previous version | s3.policy-release-pipeline | s3.policy-decision-point, s4.request-data-gate, s3.local-enforcement-point, s3.protected-audit, s3.policy-release-pipeline |
| ServiceIndicator | Indicator id (approval latency, grant success, intake-to-verified, evidence durability, posture freshness, canary freshness, draw and drill freshness), source part, window, good and total events or freshness age, observed-at. Missing data reads as unknown. Also carries postmortem trigger events as they happen: an effect outside an approved scope or with no approval, a closure contradicted by a later confirmed escape on its path key, and a seeded proposal or change that reached execution or merged, each with references to its records. | at-least-once: keyed by indicator, source and window; missing windows read as unknown | s3.policy-decision-point, s5.evidence-intake-validator, s3.gate-failure-policy, s7.sampling-service, s7.failover-drill, s7.evaluation-operations, s3.protected-audit, s5.finding-ledger | s7.reliability-monitor |
| ErrorBudgetStatus | Objective id, budget remaining over the rolling window, burn rate, freeze flag, exception id when the security risk owner allowed a release during a freeze, paging target role. | latest-wins record: objective id plus window; freeze state changes are recorded with actor | s7.reliability-monitor | s3.policy-release-pipeline, people.control-plane-operator |
| ModelResponse | Raw model output for one attempt: attempt id, route, model version, content (untrusted), usage. Never authority; it reaches producers only after the response-proposal gate validates it against the proposal schema. Carries the outsider label set of the request it answers, which the request-data gate records under the attempt id. | at-most-once per attempt: attempt id; untrusted content; never retried into a different attempt | s4.inference-endpoints, ext.hosted-frontier-endpoint | s4.response-proposal-gate |
| FeedbackProposal | Proposed change to context facts or rules from evaluation: target, proposed value, evidence references, author, expiry.
